# Commands · D · part 6

1,413 entries, doctor to dw-flow. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **doctor**: Health-check the council models on your LiteLLM proxy and self-heal a dead one. Probes each configured model with retries (a NIM id can hit end-of-life / 410 Gone, get pulled / 404, or turn too slow to use), and when one is genuinely DEAD (404/410) it offers healthy replacements from the live catalog, asks which to swap in, and saves the new council - while a rejected key, a rate limit or a proxy 5xx is reported as such instead of condemning the models. Reads LITELLM_BASE_URL / LITELLM_API_KEY / LITELLM_COUNCIL_MODELS. Node, no MCP. ([Arbuzov/claude-loadout](https://github.com/Arbuzov/claude-loadout/blob/HEAD/plugins/litellm-council/commands/doctor.md) · ★1)
- **doctor**: Health-check telegram-notify. Verifies the bot token with getMe, then checks every configured target with getChat - so it proves each chat is reachable WITHOUT sending anyone a test message. Distinguishes a bad token from a missing chat from a blocked bot, and names the fix for each. Pass --send to deliver a real test message to every target instead. ([Arbuzov/claude-loadout](https://github.com/Arbuzov/claude-loadout/blob/HEAD/plugins/telegram-notify/commands/doctor.md) · ★1)
- **doctor**: Health-check the NVIDIA council models and self-heal a dead one. Probes each configured model with retries (a NIM id can hit end-of-life / 410 Gone, get pulled / 404, or turn too slow to use), and when one is genuinely DEAD (404/410) it offers healthy replacements from the live NVIDIA catalog, asks which to swap in, and saves the new council - while a rejected key, a rate limit or a 5xx is reported as such instead of condemning the models. Reads NVIDIA_API_KEY / NVIDIA_COUNCIL_MODELS. Node, no MCP. ([Arbuzov/claude-loadout](https://github.com/Arbuzov/claude-loadout/blob/HEAD/plugins/nvidia-council/commands/doctor.md) · ★1)
- **doctor**: Check that all codebase-intelligence prerequisites (system tools, MCP servers, KB engine, vendored tools) are present, and print the exact fix for anything missing. ([arturgomes/claude-code-toolkit](https://github.com/arturgomes/claude-code-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/codebase-intelligence/commands/doctor.md) · ★1)

- **dw-brainstorm**: `/dw-brainstorm` roda **FULL** por padrão.
